````text
  const region=page.getByRole('region',{name:'Presentación de entrega',exact:true});await region.getByLabel('Número de serie observado').fill('SERIAL-V403');await region.getByRole('checkbox',{name:'Revisé el estado del activo'}).check();await region.getByLabel('Expediente de inspección').selectOption({label:'Inspección técnica verificada'});return region.getByRole('button',{name:'Confirmar revisión',exact:true});
 }
 try{const submit=await prepare();const revoked=JSON.parse(original);revoked.tenants.tenant.organizations.store[{quote:'variants',lead:'assignees',availability:'resources',checklist:'evidence'}[flow]]=[];writeFileSync(configPath,JSON.stringify(revoked));const response=page.waitForResponse(r=>r.url().endsWith('/api/enterprise/franchise/commands')&&r.request().method()==='POST');await submit.click();const rejected=await response;expect(rejected.status()).toBe(403);expect((await rejected.json()).operation_effect).toBe('NOT_ATTEMPTED');await expect(page.getByText('Las opciones cambiaron antes del envío.',{exact:false})).toBeVisible();expect(await page.evaluate(()=>Object.keys(sessionStorage))).toEqual([]);const stats=await(await context.request.get('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/stats')).json();expect(stats.writes).toHaveLength(0);
 writeFileSync(configPath,original);await page.reload();const next=await prepare();const success=page.waitForResponse(r=>r.url().endsWith('/api/enterprise/franchise/commands')&&r.request().method()==='POST');await next.click();expect((await success).status()).toBe(["quote","availability"].includes(flow)?201:200);const final=await(await context.request.get('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/stats')).json();expect(final.writes).toHaveLength(1);
 }finally{writeFileSync(configPath,original);await context.request.post('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/reset');}
});

test('focus panes preserve unfinished choices and keep one active task',async({page,context})=>{await login(context);await page.goto('/franchise');await openContact(page);await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Cotización',exact:true}).click();await page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true}).selectOption('variant-v403');await page.getByLabel('Lista de precios',{exact:true}).selectOption('retail-v403');const form=page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true}).locator('xpath=ancestor::form');await form.locator('input[type="datetime-local"]').fill('2030-01-01T10:00');await page.getByLabel('Trabajo a realizar').selectOption({label:'Disponibilidad'});await expect(page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true})).toBeHidden();await page.getByLabel('Trabajo a realizar').selectOption({label:'Contactos'});await expect(page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true})).toHaveValue('variant-v403');await expect(form.locator('input[type="datetime-local"]')).toHaveValue('2030-01-01T10:00');expect(await page.evaluate(()=>Object.keys(sessionStorage))).toEqual([]);});
test('English private interface translates task labels and preserves backend enum values',async({browser})=>{const context=await browser.newContext({locale:'en-US',ignoreHTTPSErrors:true});try{await login(context);const page=await context.newPage();await page.goto(base+'/franchise');await expect(page.getByRole('heading',{name:'Contacts',exact:true})).toBeVisible();await openContact(page);await expect(page.getByLabel('Assignee',{exact:true})).toBeVisible();await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Status',exact:true}).click();const option=page.locator('select[name="target"] option[value="contacted"]');await expect(option).toHaveText('Contacted');await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Quote',exact:true}).click();await expect(page.getByLabel('Product',{exact:true})).toBeEnabled();await expect(page.getByLabel('Price list',{exact:true})).toBeEnabled();await page.goto(base+'/experience/operations');await expect(page.getByRole('heading',{name:'Prepare delivery',exact:true})).toBeVisible();await expect(page.getByRole('button',{name:'Confirm review',exact:true})).toBeVisible();}finally{await context.close();}});

async function openContact(page){const control=page.locator('.contactWork>summary').first();if(await control.count()&&!(await control.locator('..').evaluate(node=>node.open))){await control.click();}}

test('dark console drawer traps focus, restores it and preserves a draft across work areas',async({page,context})=>{
 await login(context);await page.goto('/franchise');await expect(page.getByRole('heading',{name:'Contactos',exact:true})).toBeVisible();await expect(page.getByLabel('Responsable',{exact:true})).toBeHidden();await openContact(page);await page.getByRole('button',{name:'Cotización',exact:true}).click();await page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true}).selectOption('variant-v403');const form=page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true}).locator('xpath=ancestor::form');await form.locator('input[type="datetime-local"]').fill('2030-01-01T10:00');
 await page.setViewportSize({width:390,height:844});const opener=page.getByRole('button',{name:'Abrir menú',exact:true});await opener.click();const menu=page.getByRole('dialog',{name:'Menú',exact:true});await expect(menu).toBeVisible();await expect(page.getByRole('button',{name:'Cerrar menú',exact:true})).toBeFocused();
 // Native showModal makes the external document inert; click/focus cannot enter the form.
 expect(await form.locator('input[type="datetime-local"]').evaluate(node=>{node.focus();return document.activeElement===node;})).toBe(false);
 await page.keyboard.press('Shift+Tab');expect(await menu.evaluate(node=>node.contains(document.activeElement))).toBe(true);await page.keyboard.press('Tab');await expect(page.getByRole('button',{name:'Cerrar menú',exact:true})).toBeFocused();for(let i=0;i<22;i++){await page.keyboard.press('Tab');expect(await menu.evaluate(node=>node.contains(document.activeElement))).toBe(true);}
 await page.keyboard.press('Escape');await expect(menu).toBeHidden();await expect(opener).toBeFocused();
 await opener.click();await menu.getByRole('button',{name:'Agenda',exact:true}).click();await expect(menu).toBeHidden();await expect(page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true})).toBeHidden();await opener.click();await menu.getByRole('button',{name:'Gestión diaria',exact:true}).click();await expect(page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true})).toHaveValue('variant-v403');await expect(form.locator('input[type="datetime-local"]')).toHaveValue('2030-01-01T10:00');
 await opener.click();await page.setViewportSize({width:1440,height:1000});await expect(menu).toBeHidden();expect(await page.evaluate(()=>document.querySelectorAll('dialog[open]').length)).toBe(0);await expect(page.getByLabel('Producto',{exact:true})).toBeEnabled();expect(await page.evaluate(()=>Object.keys(sessionStorage))).toEqual([]);
});

test('public catalogue expands an explicit inquiry action without losing consent',async({page})=>{
 await page.goto('/models');await expect(page.getByRole('heading',{name:'Urban',exact:true})).toBeVisible();await expect(page.getByText('Bicicleta',{exact:true})).toBeVisible();const details=page.locator('.modelInquiry');await expect(details.locator('form')).toBeHidden();await details.locator('summary').click();await expect(details.locator('form')).toBeVisible();await expect(details.getByRole('checkbox')).not.toBeChecked();expect(await details.getByRole('checkbox').evaluate(node=>node.required)).toBe(true);
});

test('keyboard reader resolves only the complete authorized order and never submits on Enter',async({page,context})=>{
 await context.request.post('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/reset');await context.request.post('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/unit-mode?mode=complete');await login(context,{permissions:['supply:read','supply:receive']});await page.goto('/supply?order=supplyOrders-v403');await page.getByLabel('Orden de abastecimiento',{exact:true}).selectOption('supplyOrders-v403');await page.getByLabel('Orden de abastecimiento',{exact:true}).locator('xpath=ancestor::form').locator('button').click();const picker=page.getByRole('region',{name:'Buscar una unidad'});await expect(picker).toBeVisible();const code=picker.getByLabel('Código de unidad',{exact:true});await code.fill('001aB');await code.press('Enter');await expect(code).toHaveValue('001aB');await expect(picker.getByRole('button',{name:'Elegir esta unidad'})).toBeVisible();let stats=await(await context.request.get('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/stats')).json();expect(stats.writes).toHaveLength(0);await picker.getByRole('button',{name:'Elegir esta unidad'}).click();await expect(picker.getByRole('button',{name:'Unidad elegida'})).toBeDisabled();await code.press('Enter');await expect(picker.getByRole('button',{name:'Unidad elegida'})).toBeDisabled();expect((await(await context.request.get('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/stats')).json()).writes).toHaveLength(0);
 await code.fill('001ab');await code.press('Enter');await expect(picker.getByRole('status')).toContainText('No hay una unidad coincidente');await expect(picker.getByRole('button',{name:'Elegir esta unidad'})).toHaveCount(0);
 for(const mode of ['partial','ambiguous']){await context.request.post('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/unit-mode?mode='+mode);await page.reload();await page.getByLabel('Orden de abastecimiento',{exact:true}).locator('xpath=ancestor::form').locator('button').click();await code.fill('001aB');await code.press('Enter');await expect(picker.getByRole('status')).toContainText(mode==='partial'?'parcial':'más de una unidad');await expect(picker.getByRole('button',{name:'Elegir esta unidad'})).toHaveCount(0);}
 await context.request.post('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/unit-mode?mode=complete');
});

test('serial reader Enter never confirms a ready delivery and preserves the literal observation',async({page,context})=>{
 await context.request.post('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/reset');await login(context);await page.goto('/experience/operations');const region=page.getByRole('region',{name:'Presentación de entrega',exact:true});const serial=region.getByLabel('Número de serie observado');await serial.fill('001aB');await region.getByRole('checkbox',{name:'Revisé el estado del activo'}).check();await region.getByLabel('Expediente de inspección').selectOption({label:'Inspección técnica verificada'});await expect(region.getByRole('button',{name:'Confirmar revisión',exact:true})).toBeEnabled();await serial.press('Enter');await expect(serial).toHaveValue('001aB');expect((await(await context.request.get('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/stats')).json()).writes).toHaveLength(0);await serial.fill(' 001aB');expect(await serial.evaluate(node=>node.checkValidity())).toBe(false);await serial.fill('001aB');await region.getByRole('button',{name:'Confirmar revisión',exact:true}).click();const stats=await(await context.request.get('http://127.0.0.1:4314/fixture/stats')).json();expect(stats.writes).toHaveLength(1);expect(stats.completion.responses[0].response_text).toBe('001aB');
});
````

## 6. Configuration surface

No additional configuration surface beyond overlay apply flags. BUSINESS_CONFIGURATION_REQUIRED when tenant bindings absent.

## 7. Dependency bill

Stacks declared in Metadata. Companion transport required before document UI build.

## 8. Apply order

1) materialize 2) apply_overlay.py --target . --report qualification/ui-overlay.json 3) --verify 4) install/tsc/tests/build

## 9. Verification

`pnpm install --offline --frozen-lockfile --ignore-scripts`; `pnpm exec tsc --noEmit`; focused tests in `src/design/experience.v403.test.ts`, `src/platform/experience/selection.v403.test.ts`, original command route tests; `pnpm exec next build --webpack`. Portable browser harness: `python experience_overlay/qualification/run_qualification.py --target . --evidence <local-directory> --candidate` after the standalone build. It creates ephemeral loopback keys outside source, exercises actual Next BFF against AUTHORED HTTP fixtures, and retains a hash-bound receipt. Run without --candidate to compare the captured candidates; comparison is never human approval. Browser fixture receipts and candidate screenshots are linked by FRANCHISE_EXPERIENCE_PACK_PLAN_V403. Automated contrast/reflow/keyboard do not prove screen-reader, physical phone or human acceptance. Cloud execution is NOT_RUN here.

## 10. Reconstruction evidence

Reconstruction evidence is materialization sha256 locks plus qualification receipts; this pack does not claim production admission.
